00021: def beam(parent,name,a,b,width,depth,color):
00022:     dx,dy,dz=(b[i]-a[i] for i in range(3))
00023:     length=math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz)
00024:     q=box((width,depth,length),center=tuple((a[i]+b[i])/2 for i in range(3)),color=color)
00025:     q.turn('y',math.degrees(math.acos(dz/length)))
00026:     q.turn('z',math.degrees(math.atan2(dy,dx)))
00027:     return add(parent,name,q)

00020: def add(parent, name, node):
00021:     parent.include(name, node)
00022:     return node
00023: 
00024: def beam(parent, name, a, b, width, depth, color):
00025:     dx,dy,dz = (b[i]-a[i] for i in range(3))
00026:     length = math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz)
00027:     q = box((width,depth,length), center=tuple((a[i]+b[i])/2 for i in range(3)),color=color)
00028:     q.turn('y', math.degrees(math.acos(dz/length)))
00029:     q.turn('z', math.degrees(math.atan2(dy,dx)))
00030:     return add(parent,name,q)
